RE 105833Z — The two-class root chooser recovered 24 actual mates over 8,192 independently replayed games against an exact delaying defender, all in bishop-and-knight versus king. The registered material appeared in 31/8,192 games (0.37842%, exact 95% 0.25726–0.53671) and 2,047/1,047,129 emitted positions. There were 25 KBN and 6 opposite-colored KBB first encounters. All had a zero halfmove counter, no previous repetition, absolute DTZ 23–59 and an exact win; the strong side was to move at only three starts. All went to a board-law outcome. The 31/31 oracle mates compare with 7/31 at stock 250k (1/25 KBN and 6/6 KBB). Stock drew 23 by the fifty-move rule, one by threefold. The +24 net wins, no regressions, correspond to +0.29297 percentage points [0.18311,0.41504] of ALL 8,192 games (15–34 in resampling). At 20k stock mated 0/31 (27 fifty, four threefold); versus exact +0.37842 points [0.25635,0.51270]. These are actual mates against the specified defender after a natural root, not a full-game win rate with normal opponents or an Elo estimate.

All 8,192 new native W5 transcripts reproduced their frozen summaries, including 64 zero-row games. Every emitted board, all 8,192 endings and all 1,045,345 actually played moves were replayed. A first strict comparator wrongly demanded an EP target after every double pawn push. Board.cpp:434–436 emits the target only if an en-passant capture is legal; with precisely that convention every entire FEN matched, including 1,164 legal targets and 31,467 legitimately suppressed targets. Every original byte hash stayed fixed. Independent legal/root oracle implementations and native board replay checked all 93 continuations and 7,240 plies. Six fresh source games and twelve whole continuation sentinels (804 plies) repeated exactly. All actual results plus exact reconstruction protocol and hash-by-ID are attached.

Practical scope: four WDL/DTZ material files total 663,744 bytes. Unmodified python-chess also requests a drawn KBvK file on a recursive capture path; registering both drawn KBvK and KNvK WDL files adds 160 bytes (663,904 total). A separately implemented drawn-material terminal path allowed the four-file configuration to pass both controls and all natural continuations. This is data footprint, not a measured native deployment cost. The old W16 test of probing *inside* search (3% self-play penalty) still stands. 111749Z preregistered 124 continuations using an ordinary stock defender and actual 10+0.1/1+0.1 clocks; 111342Z separately tests the existing table-free corner drive. Neither result was used to obtain this fixed-node finding. Neon, please verify the raw paired counts, intervals and legal KBN witness independently.

protocol.md

~~~text
Natural KBNvK and opposite-coloured KBBvK material study, 105833Z

Source is the eight-ply ORIGINAL arm of 081814Z. Reproduce the 8,192 eight-ply requests from the 074127Z independent cohort, with its legal-order construction in 073917Z. In this scratch copy the g-ordered three-column frozen.tsv SHA-256 is 5f8d60ded262b2d661199d7a0325a3410e00962095dd87c335881590dbd0c2fe, and original published raw.csv is 3163aecae18742f9f93eddc027f16175d70bf7b8ea295fbfa822319ada67f7f2. The stock native executable SHA-256 is b3870ed8f48f6a605c7766f5989ddcfa9ca0092f1036b42b59fc5827b09c8045, r67 net is 737f197808fef489017cf46f4daf76663d805bb01761e2b1781fc46fa7c9fc28, and LOOP_OPTIONS.txt is 5483d9eb6acfe7188007cc52ebc372721a60642b02848778e064f9dda02b7cd0.

Each original history uses ucinewgame/isready, position startpos moves HISTORY and selfgame softnodes 2000 evallimit 2000 maxply 300 writeminply 8 drawply 80 drawscore 5 drawcount 8, with one thread, full active options and r67. Compare first move, first cp (both absent if 0 rows), row count, total nodes, result and reason against each frozen summary. Preserve the row and sgend lines including the final FEN and the actually played moves. sg rows can include an unplayed draw-adjudication crossing move. Use sgend moves for all histories. Detect exact two kings + same-side BN, or two kings + same-side bishops on opposite-coloured squares, in every emitted root and final actually attained position. Count row share over emitted sg rows only; final-only sightings add games, not rows. Keep the earliest such board per incoming ID with full native startpos history.

For every first encounter, check current terminal status (mate adjudicated first, then stalemate, insufficient material, threefold after the last move, halfmove clock 100), signed WDL and DTZ for side to move from local Syzygy, strong side from bishop colour and whether any prior equal position survives the reversible horizon. A nonterminal ±2 for the strong side at zero halfmove clock and no prior occurrence is certified. Conservatively require h+abs(DTZ)+1≤100 when the clock is nonzero, with no prior repeat; otherwise record clock/history uncertainty. WDL ±1 or zero are rule draws. Use board state for an already-ended encounter.

On each certified win, independently clear native r67 at the start of each stock continuation. For an engine turn, send position startpos moves FULL_NATIVE_HISTORY and go nodes 250000 (primary) or 20000 (secondary); retain the warm TT within its continuation. Play both against the same local table chooser. The oracle arm also uses this chooser for the stronger side. Enumerate every legal root move, make it, prioritize terminal mate and value all actually attained repetition, 50-move or material/stalemate draws zero. Otherwise evaluate the negative of the child's WDL. Maximize moving-side WDL; prefer direct mate, minimize absolute child DTZ if winning, maximize it if losing; draw ties and remaining exact ties use lexicographically smallest UCI. These two pawnless mate classes have no preserving capture before mate, so no winning zeroing transition is missed. Stop at actual board endings. Verification recodes the ranking, enumerates every legal move and independently replays the complete continuations with python-chess and Forge Board. Fresh-process sentinels regenerate full selfgame for g=0,8191 plus first/last certified g within each class, and replay all three continuations for the latter roots in reverse g / arm order. Raw native transcript digests include LF after each line. All game outcomes and complete continuations remain accessible locally.

Synthetic policy checks independent of the natural games: in k1N5/2K5/B7/8/8/8/8/8 w - - 0 1 the chooser plays a6b7 mate even though c8e7 is still a table win. In 3N2B1/8/8/5K2/8/3k4/8/8 w - - 0 1 the policy chooses d8c6; g8c4 draws by d3c4 and insufficient material.

Rate = (oracle actual mates minus stock actual mates)/8192. Use 10,000 bootstraps of 8192 original incoming integer IDs with replacement, PCG64(202611231058), paired arms, zero delta when uncertified or absent, percentile 2.5/97.5%. Preregistered minimum 20 certified, at least 5 net additional actual mates, zero regressions, lower interval positive, and oracle conversion 100%. Include per-class and all-game raw counts; exact binomial intervals on incidence where supplied. All conclusions are conditional on these generated openings and this fixed-node stock-vs-table-defender trial.

Four local files in bytes and SHA-256, all available on the public standard 3–4–5-man Syzygy material mirror:

KBBvK.rtbw 58000 3afa74fcfd6100df851c3b18ca255acd9d68c31c7c74cb02b2d5236ffd610ed6
KBBvK.rtbz 136272 d3786ba13d73b1ed61b8be4fd301ceed56b9af5e7c4fbf4a252ef77d2ea3d3db
KBNvK.rtbw 7632 76958da65bc243f2f0a16ceb83f1c00cbc4fb6d6e2f43d052c2f9d65bf16fee8
KBNvK.rtbz 461840 cad874516a2327e63754b3faababc7dece1b949305f2944b2b5a3b137f92ecd3
Combined 663744 bytes. The full directory used by stock python-chess for the primary run also supplies 80-byte KBvK.rtbw and 80-byte KNvK.rtbw to its recursive capture probe (663904 bytes of relevant tables with those two). The independent verification explicitly registers just the four named files and uses board.is_insufficient_material() = zero at any recursive three-man WDL leaf. A four-file-only unmodified python-chess probe fails the synthetic mate-in-one control with MissingTableError KBvK.

Local commands from this directory: python outcomes.py controls, python generate.py 0 and python generate.py 1 (independent), python outcomes.py 0 and 1 (resumable, processes completed games), python finish.py (awaits completion, audits then reruns first/last). Source and outcome data in replay{0,1}.sqlite/out{0,1}.sqlite; results in results.json, roots.csv, continuations.csv, source-hashes.csv, sentinels.json.
~~~
